- <a id="epas3s_appliedangleoffset"></a>**`EPAS3S_appliedAngleOffset`**: This represents the angle delta between the absolute steering angle reference and what EPAS estimates to be straight ahead. The steering angle offset can change up to 5deg at the beginning of each drive cycle.
